# STLSoft - Components Inventory (working ledger) <!-- omit in toc -->

This is the working ledger of the components of **STLSoft** 1.11.1: every substantive component introduced in 1.10.x and 1.11.x, the legacy components that matter (used by other STLSoft components, used by other Synesis projects, or nominated on analysis), and a full per-file and per-sub-project inventory of **include/**. It is the source of truth for **COMPONENTS.md** and for the Components tables in **README.md**. It was generated on 19th October 2026 from the **package-and-documentation** working tree at tag **1.11.1-rc9**; components arriving in rc10 will be added when they land.

## Method

* **Unit** — a header stem (`.h` + `.hpp` pair collapsed; the **(+N)** marks extra files in the group). Class-/function-level grouping (see decision 1) is a manual refinement of this file-level first pass;
* **Support headers** (paths containing `internal/`, `api/`, `util/`, `implicit_link`, `fwd.h`, `*stl.h`, `*_.h`, `std/`, `ver/`, `config/`, `compiler/`) are counted separately and **not** nominated for COMPONENTS.md;
* **Since** — first tag in the **STLSoft.b** history containing the header, following `git` renames (`-M50%`). Tag history begins at **1.9.118**, so every pre-existing header shows **≤1.9.118**; earlier provenance would come from **CHANGES.txt** / header banners and is deferred;
* **C** — direct `#include` count from other headers under **include/** (threshold ≥ 3; the count is shown). Tests/examples excluded;
* **D** — `#include <stlsoft|platformstl|…/>` from non-test, non-example sources in other freelibs projects and the **~/dev/sistools** tools (excluding **STLSoft/**, build trees); the project names are listed. Includes from **test/**, **examples/**, **scratch** are held apart as a weaker signal;
* **E** — my judgement, each with a one-line rationale; flagged for accept/reject;
* **B** — all non-support headers first appearing in **1.10.x** or **1.11.x**. "Significant enhancement" items (not new headers) are in [Other signals](#other-signals) as raw **CHANGES.md** candidates.


## Summary

| Set | Count |
| --- | ---: |
| All header stems under **include/** | 951 |
| Support stems (excluded) | 210 |
| B1 new in 1.11.x (non-support) | 22 |
| B2 new in 1.10.x (non-support) | 180 |
| Legacy (≤1.9.118) non-support | 485 |
| of which C (≥3 STLSoft includers) | 95 |
| of which D (used by other SIS projects) | 117 (of which 6 weak: tests/examples only) |
| of which C or D | 171 |
| of which E-only (nominated, not C/D) | 100 |
| Legacy, none of C/D/E | 214 |
| Obsolete/deprecated paths among legacy | 13 |

### 1.10 / 1.11 enhancement candidates from CHANGES.md

Bullets matching performance / optimisation / enhancement keywords, for the operator to select "significant enhancements" for the README table.


* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: Approximate measured gains for `platformstl::basic_file_lines<>` on the new harness (1000×64 LF): view-based forms (~`stlsoft::string_view` / `std::string_view`) about **10–20×** faster than `fgetc` line accumulation and typically **comparable to or slightly ahead of** `std::getline` (~1.2× on MinGW CI); owning-string forms remain competitive with `std::getline` while avoiding the extra full-file copy;
* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: CI — performance job exports **SIS_PERFTESTS_GROUPGAPS=1**;
* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: **execute_performance_tests.sh** — capture the child exit code after optional `expand` piping (was overwriting a failed status with `$?` from the success branch);
* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: Test tree — leaf directories under **test/component**, **test/unit**, **test/scratch**, and **test/performance** renamed from `test.<kind>.…` to the subject name (for example **glob_sequence**); executable names stay `test.<kind>.*`;
* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: **test/performance** — programs nested by sub-project and area (**platformstl/diagnostics**, **platformstl/filesystem**, **stlsoft/containers**, **stlsoft/conversion**, **stlsoft/diagnostics**, **stlsoft/memory**, **stlsoft/string**, **stlsoft/util**);
* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: Performance suite **test/performance/stlsoft/util/bit_functions** (density patterns, intrinsic rows, median / ns-per-call reporting);
* **1.11.1-rc7 - 30th September 2026**: Performance suite **test/performance/platformstl/filesystem/file_lines** — comparative harness vs `fgetc` / `std::getline` (LF / CRLF / CR; mid-size and small-file shapes; C++11+);
* **1.11.1-rc6 - 21st September 2026**: Scripts — **.sis/project_name.txt**; colourised **run_all_***.sh / **execute_performance_tests.sh**; new **ctest_cmake.sh**; MSYS auto-MinGW in **prepare_cmake.sh**;
* **1.11.1-rc4 - 29th July 2026**: Test/example runner scripts — **--unit-only** / **--component-only** on **run_all_unit_tests.sh**; new **run_all_examples.sh** / **.cmd**; **run_all_unit_tests.cmd**, **run_all_scratch_tests.cmd**, and **execute_performance_tests.cmd** aligned with shell counterparts;
* **1.11.1-rc2 - 31st May 2025**: Significant improvements to compatibility with different C and C++ language versions (C90, C99, C11, C17, C23, C++98, C++11, C++14, C++17, C++20, C++23) - detailed in TODO.md;
* **1.11.1-rc1 - 6th May 2025**: Various CMake improvements;
* **1.11.1-beta8 - 3rd May 2025**: Various example improvements;
* **1.11.1-beta6 - 28th April 2025**: Refactored / enhanced retrieval of home-directory, including, for Windows, trying first for `"USERPROFILE"` and then falls back to `"HOMEDRIVE"` and `"HOMEPATH"`;
* **1.11.1-beta2 - 23rd February 2025**: **InetSTL** improved compatibility with macOS / Linux;
* **1.11.1-beta1 - 31st December 2024**: Command-line handling components minor improvements and unit-testing;
* **1.11.1-alpha28 - 28th December 2024**: substantial reworking of abstraction of memory functions, resulting in performance improvements across numerous components, `stlsoft::auto_buffer<>` in particular;
* **1.11.1-alpha27 - 19th December 2024**: added **test.performance.stlsoft.count_digits**;
* **1.11.1-alpha27 - 19th December 2024**: added **test.performance.stlsoft.doomgram**;
* **1.11.1-alpha27 - 19th December 2024**: added **test.performance.stlsoft.gram_utils**;
* **1.11.1-alpha27 - 19th December 2024**: miscellaneous minor/trivial improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha24 - 4th November 2024**: **test.performance.stlsoft.frequency_map** : ~ improved reporting of results ; ~ now uses `stlsoft::std_chrono_hrc_stopwatch`;
* **1.11.1-alpha23 - 28th October 2024**: Minor improvements of ./CMakeLists.txt;
* **1.11.1-alpha17 - 13th October 2024**: `stlsoft::frequency_map<>` enhancements;
* **1.11.1-alpha17 - 13th October 2024**: `unixstl::glob_sequence` enhancements;
* **1.11.1-alpha17 - 13th October 2024**: Fixes and enhancement to `dl_call<>()` (both UNIXSTL and WinSTL);
* **1.11.1-alpha17 - 13th October 2024**: General improvements and simplification for compatibility with C++11 - C++20;
* **1.11.1-alpha17 - 13th October 2024**: CMake build files improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha17 - 13th October 2024**: Misc. minor fixes and enhancements;
* **1.11.1-alpha16 - 1st October 2024**: `auto_buffer` improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha16 - 1st October 2024**: Time function enhancements;
* **1.11.1-alpha16 - 1st October 2024**: Boilerplate file improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha16 - 1st October 2024**: Miscellaneous improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha15 - 2nd September 2024**: minor additions/enhancements to scratch tests;
* **1.11.1-alpha8 - 9th July 2024**: GCC compatibility improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha8 - 9th July 2024**: CMake improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha8 - 9th July 2024**: Boilerplate file improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha4 - 18th March 2024**: various performance improvements;
* **1.11.1-alpha1 - 20th February 2024**: improvement to string insertion helper `string_insert()` to work with stream types that do and do not provide `sentry`;
* **1.10.10 - 16th February 2024**: fixes and enhancements to `stlsoft::util::string_insert`;
* **1.10.7 - 30th January 2024**: enhanced stream insertion operators, when used with types descended from `std::basic_ios<>`, to observe width and alignment for `stlsoft::basic_shim_string<>`, `stlsoft::basic_simple_string<>`, `stlsoft::basic_static_string`, `stlsoft::basic_string_view`, `stlsoft::stlsoft_C_string_slice_m_t`/`stlsoft::stlsoft_C_string_slice_w_t`;
* **1.10.6 - 28th January 2024**: simplification and improvement of CMake handling;
* **1.10.2 - 3rd January 2024**: MinGW 64-bit compatibility improvements;
* **1.10.1 beta 26 - 4th January 2021**: (UNIXSTL and WinSTL) basic_path<> added move semantics and get_location() method and made swap() method public, as well as numerous defect fixes and consistency improvements implemented in terms of new Path Classification API;
* **1.10.1 beta 26 - 4th January 2021**: many improvements to GCC compatibility;
* **1.10.1 beta 22 - 29th November 2020**: stlsoft::split() functions enhanced to: return vector of arbitrary length; allow heterogeneous types for the split elements;
* **1.10.1 beta 18 - 31st October 2019**: added stlsoft/system/environment/functions.hpp to enhances stlsoft::environment_variable_exists() with string access shims to support arbitrary string type;
* **1.10.1 beta 17 - 24th October 2019**: improved documentation;
* **1.10.1 beta 15 - 13th September 2019**: various portability improvements to newer compilers;
* **1.10.1 beta 5 - 15th May 2017**: enhanced STLSOFT_SUPPRESS_UNUSED() to work with rvalues;
* **1.10.1 beta 2 - 28th January 2017**: a large number of changes to improve compatibility with UNIX, Clang, GCC, and MinGW;
* **1.10.1 beta 1 - 14th January 2017**: new high-performance conversion functions (based around the extant technology in stlsoft::integer_to_string()): integer_to_decimal_string(), integer_to_hexadecimal_string(), integer_to_octal_string(), integer_to_lc_string(), integer_to_zero_padded_decimal_string();
* **1.10.1 beta 1 - 14th January 2017**: substantially improved atomic function support;
* **1.10.1 beta 1 - 14th January 2017**: substantially improved spin_mutex class templates and policies;
* **1.10.1 beta 1 - 14th January 2017**: ... and many many other improvements and additions ..;


## Full inventory

### Files

One row per file under **include/**, in path order. Generated mechanically: **lib** from the Doxygen library group (else the directory); **abs level** by heuristic; **desc** from `\brief`/`Purpose:`.

Every **abs level** of class / class template / C++ API / C API was inferred from header keywords, not verified;

The formerly 40 unresolved **abs level** cells were assigned by reading each header, introducing: **Fwd Decl** (forward declarations only); **Macros** (preprocessor symbols / constants); **Types** (enums and typedefs); **Umbrella** (aggregating includes only); plus existing **Int Help** (feature discrimination, common includes) and **Compat** (forwarding stubs). Accept, rename or merge;

Empty files (`stlsoft/memory/counting_allocator_adaptor.hpp`, a zero-byte file since 1.11.1-alpha21, and `stlsoft/util/std/utility.hpp`, which defines nothing) show status `empty` and are omitted from the sub-project tables;

An obsolete badge is derived from the path or from "obsolete"/"deprecated" in the brief;

Provenance: git tags begin at 1.9.118, so legacy since-badges are *estimates*: each is the first release in **CHANGES.md** (1.9.1-beta1, 20 Dec 2005, to 1.9.118, 31 May 2014) dated on or after the `Created:` date in the header banner; a header created before 20 Dec 2005 shows `≤1.9.1-beta1`; a header with no `Created:` date, or one later than 1.9.118 (reorganised or renamed files), keeps `≤1.9.118`. These badges link to **CHANGES.md**, not a tag (no tag exists). Since-badge colours by MAJOR.MINOR era: ≤1.9.118 dark grey (`404040`); 1.9.119+ orange (`d97706`); 1.10+ blue (`0969da`); 1.11+ green (`1a7f37`). Extra categories used: **Compat** (backwards-compatible forwarding header).

### Sub-projects

Component tables, one per sub-project (STLSoft first, then the rest case-insensitively). Excluded as non-components: **int-hlpr**, **fac(int)**, **fac(ext)**, **Compat**. Rows are per-file (names extracted mechanically); grouping into families is manual refinement.

**enhanced** = latest tag at which the file's `*_MAJOR`/`*_MINOR` macro changed (`-` = unchanged since 1.9.118).

**min lang** is the compile floor of the header: the lowest standard under which `clang -fsyntax-only` accepts a translation unit that includes it (C++98 / C++11 / C++14 / C++17 / C++20; C89 / C99 / C11 under `-pedantic-errors`), raised to the highest *unguarded* language requirement found by source analysis (unconditional C++11+ standard-library includes and keywords outside feature guards). `long long` is treated as a universal extension. `(C++NN optional)` marks C++NN features used only behind `STLSOFT_CF_*` / `__cplusplus` guards, with a pre-NN fallback. The compile was done on macOS (Apple clang 21, with `-pthread -D_REENTRANT`), so Windows, COM, ATL, MFC, WTL, ACE and Internet-API headers cannot be compiled here and carry `†` (source analysis only: no unguarded C++11+ construct found). Tests were consulted only for build configuration; no test program was run.

**7-Signs** abbreviations:
* *Corr* = Correctness/Reliability/Robustness;
* *Eff* = Efficiency;
* *DnT* = Discoverability/Transparency;
* *Mod* = Modularity;
* *Expr* = Expressiveness;
* *Flex* = Flexibility;
* *Port* = Portability;

Abstraction Level abbreviations:
* `class` = regular C++ class at the API level;
* `class<T>` = C++ class-template at the API level;
* **C++ API** = C++ function(s) and/or function template(s) at the API level;
* **C API** = C function(s) at the API level;
* int-hlpr = internal helper (constants, macros, functions, enumerations, classes, ...);
* fac(int) = internal façade(e);
* fac(ext) = external façade(e);
